Coordinate pass algorithm:
```
def cd_pass(h,n,deadline,g grid=33):
  h_work=h.copy(); c=correlate(h_work,1-h_work); improved=False
  for p in range(n):
    if time>deadline: break
    h0=h_work[p]
    if h0<=1e-12 or h0>=1-1e-12: continue? We can still change to bound? def cd_exact(h,n,deadline,max_passes=20, grid=33):
  h=h.copy(); c=correlate; Cmax=c.max()*dx? We use C_c = c.max(); dx separate.
  best_h=h; best_Cc=Cmax
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time>deadline: break
    h_work=h.copy(); c_work=c.copy(); curr_max=Cmax; improved=False
    for p in range(n):
      if time>deadline: return best...
      h0=h_work[p]
      if h0 < -1e-12 or h0>1+... not; clip
      K=max(p,n-1-p); m_lo=n-1-K; m_hi=n-1+K; L=m_hi-m_lo+1
      base=c_work[m_lo:m_hi+1]
      r=(n-1)-np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
      a=np.zeros(L)
      fp=p+r; vf=(fp>=0)&(fp<n)
      if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h_work[fp[vf]]
      bp=p-r; vb=(bp>=0)&(bp<n)
      if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h_work[bp[vb]]
      idx0 = (n-1) - m_lo
      # const: max outside
      # precompute prefix/suffix arrays for c_work once per coordinate? c_work changes, but outside of p. We can compute with np.max(c_work[:m_lo]) and np.max(c_work[m_hi+1:]) each coordinate O(n). Better precompute prefix max? c changes over pass, but before p we can use c_work. Could compute as needed; n=700, 700 coords ->490k max ops, fine. Use if m_lo>0 else -inf; if m_hi<2n-2 else -inf.
      const = max(...)
      f_curr = max(np.max(base), const)  # should equal curr_max? Maybe due c stale? Use curr_max.
      d_opt, f_opt = _coord_best_d(base,a,idx0,const,h0)
      if f_opt < curr_max - 1e-10: # or 1e-9
         h_work[p] = h0+d_opt
         newvals = base + a*d_opt
         newvals[idx0] -= d_opt*d_opt
         c_work[m_lo:m_hi+1] = newvals
         curr_max = f_opt
         improved=True
    # after pass
    if improved:
       h_proj = project_feasible(h_work,n)
       c_proj = correlate(h_proj,1-h_proj)
       Cproj=c_proj.max()
       if Cproj < best_Cc - 1e-12:
           best_h=h_proj; best_Cc=Cproj; h=h_proj; c=c_proj
       # maybe also if no improvement but near; continue?
       if not improved: break
  return best_h, best_Cc*dx
```

```
def _coord_eval5(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, d):
    if base_o.size:
       vals = base_o + a_o*d
       e = float(np.max(vals))
       if const > e: e = const
    else: e = const
    p = base0 + a0*d - d*d
    return p if p > e else e

def _coord_E(base_o,a_o,const,d): ...

    p = base0 + a0*d - d*d
    e = ...
    return p - e

`_coord_best_d(base,a,idx0,const,h0):`

   else: root_lo=lo

# Also maybe d_g if inside positive; f there p max not min.

Project exact: Need target = n/2. Since n may be odd? sum exact n/2. If n odd, target .5; still floats. Use iterative.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  target=n/2.0
  h=np.clip(h.astype(float,copy=True),0,1)
  for _ in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    if diff>0:
      mask=h<1.0-1e-12
      if not np.any(mask): break
      room=(1.0-h)[mask].sum()
      if room<=1e-15: break
      h[mask] = np.minimum(1.0, h[mask] + diff/room*(1.0-h[mask]))
    else:
      mask=h>1e-12
      if not np.any(mask): break
      mass=h[mask].sum()
      if mass<=1e-15: break
      h[mask] = np.maximum(0.0, h[mask] + diff/mass*h[mask])
  # final exact
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
    idx=np.where((h>1e-10)&(h<1.0-1e-10))[0]
    if idx.size:
      h[idx[0]] += diff
      h[idx[0]]=np.clip(h[idx[0]],0,1)
      # if clipped changed sum again, distribute to others? residual after clip tiny maybe
    else:
      # adjust all a bit? if all bounds, target infeasible? n/2 feasible; should have some interior? If binary, adjusting one to out of bounds not ok. Use small uniform:
      h += diff/n
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # For exact, can use: while diff ... tiny adjust first slack multiple
  for _ in range(10):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    idx=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12))[0]
    if idx.size:
       h[idx[0]] += diff
       h[idx[0]]=np.clip(h[idx[0]],0,1)
    else: break
  return h
```
But if adjust one by diff and clip, sum may change. Use iterative. Since diff tiny, first adjustment likely within. At final, to ensure sum within 1e-12 enough. Probably okay.

Subgradient top K details:
```
def _lag_grad(m,h,n):
  r=(n-1)-m
  g=np.zeros(n)
  if r>=0:
    g[:n-r] += 1.0 - h[r:]
    if r!=0: g[r:] -= h[:n-r]
    else: g += (1.0 - 2.0*h) # Wait for r=0: first g[:n] +=1-h, second g[0:] -=h => 1-2h. The code if r!=0 else? We can just do both with r=0: g[:n] +=1-h; g[0:] -=h. OK.
  else:
    a=-r
    g[a:] += 1.0 - h[:n-a]
    g[:a] -= h[a:]
  return g
```
This computes derivative for orientation r. Let's verify r positive: c=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_i(1-h_{i+r}). Deriv_i: for i first (i≤n-1-r): +1-h_{i+r} -> g[i]+=1-h[i+r] for i=0..n-1-r. For second: term i-r first? i=r..n-1: -h_{i-r}. Yes.
For r negative: c=∑_{i=-r}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i+r}) where r=-a, i=a..n-1: first g[i]+=1-h[i-a] for i=a..n-1; second for i=0..a-1: -h[i+a]. Good.
For r=0: first g[i]+=1-h[i]; second g[i]-=h[i]; ok.

Restart/perturbation:
```
def make_perturbed(h,n,scale,rng,mode):
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
  if mode==0: h2=h + scale*rng.normal(0,1,n)
  elif mode==1: h2=h + scale*np.sin(2*np.pi*(1+rng.rand()*3)*x + rng.rand()*2*np.pi)
  elif mode==2: h2=h*(1+0.5*scale*rng.normal(0,1,n))
  elif mode==3: block length n//10; start=int(rng.rand()*(n-block)); h2=h.copy(); h2[start:start+block]=1-h2[start:start+block]
  return project_feasible(h2,n)
```
No lambdas; top-level.
Then for each restart, `h_cand = make...`; `h_cand,c_cand = cd_exact(h_cand,n, deadline_res, max_passes=5)`; if c<best accept. Maybe also run subgrad if improve.

```
def resample_and_polish(h,n_old,n_new,deadline,dx?):
  x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new)
  h_new=project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h),n_new)
  h_new,c_new=cd_exact(h_new,n_new,deadline,max_passes=3)
  return h_new,c_new
```

Pair move p, q, d: h_p += d, h_q -= d. Bounds: d ∈ [-h_p, 1-h_q] and also for q: -d ≤ h_q ≤1 -> d∈[h_q-1, h_q]. Combined [max(-h_p, h_q-1), min(1-h_q, h_q)]. If p==q invalid.
For each affected lag m, new c_m = base + d*(lin_p - lin_q) + d^2*quad + const. Where lin coefficients as single-coordinate derivatives at current h: lin_p[m] from derivative wrt p; lin_q derivative wrt q. Since c_m quadratic bilinear; first-order plus cross term if p and q pair. Cross term coefficient qcoef=1 for each pair occurrence? Need total for c_m. The exact Δ for c_m as function of d can be computed directly from terms involving p or q:
 c_m = sum of terms T_i = h_i - h_i h_{i+r} (for valid pairs). Terms can involve 0,1,2 changed coordinates.
 If a term involves only p: Δ = d*(1-h_other) or as single.
 only q: Δ = -d*(derivative wrt q) (since q change -d) = d*(-deriv_q).
 both p and q: we can compute exact quadratic by considering the term. Cases:
  - self p (r=0 and i=p): term h_p(1-h_p): Δ = d(1-2h_p) - d^2.
  - self q: term h_q(1-h_q): Δ = -d(1-2h_q) - d^2.
  - pair p,q: term could be h_p(1-h_q) (if q=p+r) or h_q(1-h_p) (if p=q+r). For h_p +=d,h_q-=d:
     If term h_p(1-h_q): Δ = d(1 - h_q + h_p) + d^2 (from above)
     If term h_q(1-h_p): Δ = d(-1 + h_p - h_q) + d^2 (from above)
 Cross coefficient +1.
 If a lag has both p and q in same term, the linear coefficient from summing single derivatives +? Single derivatives for p and q for term h_p(1-h_q): ∂p=1-h_q, ∂q=-h_p. Combined d∂p + (-d)∂q = d(1-h_q)+d h_p = d(1-h_q+h_p). Good, matches linear; plus cross +d^2. So if we use lin = deriv_p - deriv_q and add cross=+1 for that term, exact.
 Thus for each lag m, quad coefficient = 
   -1 if p and q are both self? self p and self q are at same lag r=0: two terms, total quad -2.
   +1 for each cross term where p and q paired in that lag. For a fixed |p-q|, there may be up to two lags (r and -r), each with one cross term. So quad can be +1 for up to two lag indices, -2 at m0. If p and q adjacent? The two cross lags: r=d and r=-d (if valid). Also self if r=0? no q different.
 We can precompute arrays:
   base = c for union of affected lags of p or q.
   lin = derivative_p - derivative_q for each union lag.
   quad = zeros; set m0 += -2? But m0 included if affected (yes for p/q? r=0 affected by both, included in union). For cross: if q-p = r0 (positive), then lag with r=r0 (m=n-1-r0) has cross +1; lag with r=-r0 (m=n-1+r0) has cross +1, provided those lags are valid (they should be if pair appears in one orientation). Actually if p<q, r0=q-p>0. The pair appears in c for r=r0 as term p first (valid if q<n); and for r=-r0 as term q first (valid if p>0). Both lags have valid pair, so set both +1. If one orientation invalid? Since p,q inside 0..n-1, both pairs are valid: for r=r0, i=p valid q valid; for r=-r0, i=q valid p valid. So both. What about sum term linear for each? Yes.

Let's write `subgradient_phase` code:
```
def subgradient_phase(h,n,deadline,max_iters=1500,topk=20,step0=0.05,beta=0.7):
  h=h.copy(); c=compute_c5(); best=h.copy(); bestc=cc
  mom=np.zeros(n); step=step0; no_imp=0
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    c_arr=compute_corr(h); maxc=c_arr.max()
    curr=maxc*2.0/n
    if curr<bestc:
      best=h.copy(); bestc=curr; no_imp=0
    else:
      no_imp+=1
      if no_imp>150 and rng? Need rng not passed. Use deterministic sin noise.
    k=topk
    idx=np.argpartition(c_arr, -k)[-k:]
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for m in idx: grad += _lag_grad(m,h,n)
    grad /= k
    mom=beta*mom+(1-beta)*grad
    step = step0 / (1.0 + 0.005*it)
    if no_imp>200:
       # small sinusoidal perturbation
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       h_trial = h + 0.01*np.sin(2*np.pi*3*x)
       h_trial=project_feasible(h_trial,n)
    else: h_trial = h - step*mom
    h=project_feasible(h_trial,n)
  return best,bestc
```

Let's write `cd_exact` with optional `tol` and `grid`.
```
def cd_exact(h,n,deadline,max_passes=20,tol=1e-10):
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  c=compute_corr(h); cmax=c.max()
  best_h=h.copy(); best_cc=cmax
  dx=2.0/n
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    h_work=h.copy(); c_work=c.copy(); curr_max=cmax; improved=False
    n1=n-1; total_lags=2*n-2
    for p in range(n):
      if time.time()>deadline: return best_h,best_cc*dx
      h0=float(h_work[p])
      if h0 <= 1e-14:
        # can only increase; but maybe skip? include
      if h0 >= 1-1e-14:
        # can only decrease
      K = p if p>n1-p else n1-p? max(p, n1-p)
      m_lo=n1-K; m_hi=n1+K
      base=c_work[m_lo:m_hi+1]
      L=base.size
      r=n1 - np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
      a=np.zeros(L)
      fp=p+r; vf=(fp>=0)&(fp<n)
      if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h_work[fp[vf]]
      bp=p-r; vb=(bp>=0)&(bp<n)
      if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h_work[bp[vb]]
      idx0=(n1-m_lo)
      const=-1e300
      if m_lo>0:
        v=float(c_work[:m_lo].max()); const=max(const,v)
      if m_hi<total_lags:
        v=float(c_work[m_hi+1:].max()); const=max(const,v)
      f_curr=curr_max
      d,f_opt=_coord_best_d(base,a,idx0,const,h0)
      if f_opt < f_curr - tol:
        h_new=h0+d
        h_new=min(1,max(0,h_new))
        h_work[p]=h_new
        newvals=base + a*d
        newvals[idx0]-=d*d
        c_work[m_lo:m_hi+1]=newvals
        curr_max=f_opt
        improved=True
    if not improved: break
    h_proj=project_feasible(h_work,n)
    c_proj=compute_corr(h_proj)
    cproj_max=c_proj.max()
    if cproj_max < best_cc - 1e-12:
      best_h=h_proj.copy(); best_cc=cproj_max; h=h_proj; c=c_proj
    # If not improved, still maybe use h_proj for next pass? It might be slightly worse/better. To allow escape, if cproj_max <= cmax? But local. We'll set h/c to h_proj/c_proj only if not worse than original pass input? Actually if projection worsens vs best but maybe better than current h? current h feasible before pass. If cproj < cmax (pre-pass) even if not < best? best is <=cmax. If cproj< cmax, continue; else break? We can set h=h_proj if cproj < cmax * (1+1e-12) maybe. But monotone not required. Let's continue if cproj < cmax - 1e-12 or pass count small. Otherwise if projection worsened, revert to previous h/c (which was best or current). But if no improvement after projection, local optimum maybe break.
    if cproj_max >= best_cc - 1e-12:
       # if not improving best, break to avoid cycling
       if pass == 0 and cproj_max < cmax - 1e-9:
          h=h_proj; c=c_proj; cmax=cproj_max
       else:
          break
    else:
       cmax=cproj_max
```
This logic maybe too tangled. Simpler: Always set h,c to h_proj,c_proj after each pass; update best if cproj < best. Even if cproj slightly worse than input, next pass may improve; but could cycle. To prevent, if cproj > cmax (pre-pass) + 1e-9: break. Else continue. Good.
```
old_cmax = cmax
... pass

   # projection bad, discard and stop? 
   break
h=h_proj; c=c_proj; cmax=cproj_max

Now `_coord_best_d` implementation in detail. Need avoid too much code. Let's write robust but concise.

```
def _coord_e(base_o,a_o,const,d):
    if base_o.size:
        m = float(np.max(base_o + a_o*d))
        return m if m > const else const
    return const
def _coord_hroot(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,d):
    p = base0 + a0*d - d*d
    return p - _coord_e(base_o,a_o,const,d)
def _coord_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,d):
    e=_coord_e(...)
    p=base0+a0*d-d*d
    return e if e > p else p
```
This computes e twice in _coord_f if we call _coord_h? But OK.
For grid, use vectorized; for scalar use.

`_coord_best_d`:
```
def _coord_best_d(base,a,idx0,const,h0):
    lo=-h0; hi=1.0-h0
    if hi-lo <= 1e-14:
       return 0.0, _coord_f(...) but base_o not made. Could just f0=max(max(base),const)
    base_o=base.copy(); a_o=a.copy(); base_o[idx0]=-1e300
    base0=base[idx0]; a0=a[idx0]
    # d_g
    lo_g=lo; hi_g=hi
    for _ in range(45):
       if hi_g-lo_g < 1e-11: break
       mid=0.5*(lo_g+hi_g)
       if base_o.size:
          vals=base_o + a_o*mid
          maxline=float(np.max(vals))
          maxv=max(const,maxline)
          feas=vals >= maxv - 1e-12
          if np.any(feas): min_s=float(a_o[feas].min()); max_s=float(a_o[feas].max())
          else: min_s=max_s=0.0
       else:
          maxv=const; min_s=max_s=0.0
       if const >= maxv - 1e-12:
          if 0<min_s: min_s=0.0
          if 0>max_s: max_s=0.0
       if max_s < -1e-12: lo_g=mid
       elif min_s > 1e-12: hi_g=mid
       else: break
    dg=0.5*(lo_g+hi_g)
    best_d=0.0; best_f=_coord_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,0.0)
    for d in (dg,lo,hi):
       f=_coord_f(...,d)
       if f<best_f: best_d=d;best_f=f
    # grid
    L=base_o.size
    G=33 if L>100 else 17
    dgrid=np.linspace(lo,hi,G)
    if L:
       vals=base_o[None,:] + a_o[None,:]*dgrid[:,None]
       e_grid=np.max(vals,axis=1)
       # if const > e_grid: e_grid = np.where(e_grid>const,e_grid,const) -> e_grid[e_grid<const]=const
       if const > -1e299:
          e_grid = np.maximum(e_grid, const)
    else:
       e_grid=np.full(G,const)
    p_grid=base0+a0*dgrid-dgrid*dgrid
    f_grid=np.maximum(e_grid,p_grid)
    # update best
    ibest=int(np.argmin(f_grid))
    if f_grid[ibest]<best_f: best_d=float(dgrid[ibest]); best_f=float(f_grid[ibest])
    # roots
    if L>0 and float(np.max(p_grid-e_grid)) > 1e-14:
       h_grid=p_grid-e_grid
       imax=int(np.argmax(h_grid))
       # left
       if imax>0 and h_grid[imax-1] <= 0.0:
          root=_bisect_h_negpos(dgrid[imax-1], dgrid[imax], h_grid[imax-1], h_grid[imax], base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const)
          f=_coord_f(...,root); update
       elif h_grid[0] > 0.0:
          # positive at lo; min may be at lo or right root
          pass
       # right
       if imax<G-1 and h_grid[imax+1] <= 0.0:
          root=_bisect_h_posneg(dgrid[imax], dgrid[imax+1], h_grid[imax], h_grid[imax+1], ...)
          update
       elif h_grid[-1] > 0.0:
          pass
       # if h positive at endpoints include endpoints already evaluated
    # Maybe refine best_d using a small grid around best_d if not endpoint
    span=max(1e-6, (hi-lo)/G)
    dref=np.linspace(best_d-span,best_d+span,21); clip to [lo,hi]
    vals=... update.
    return best_d,best_f
```

Bisection helpers need passed arrays each call; that is okay.
```
def _bisect_h_negpos(left,right,h_left,h_right,base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const):
  for _ in range(35):
    mid=0.5*(left+right)
    hm=_coord_hroot(...)
    if hm > 0.0:
      left=mid
    else:
      right=mid
  return 0.5*(left+right)
```
For left root, h_left<=0,h_right>0; if hm>0, root to right => left=mid. Right root: h_left>0,h_right<=0; if hm>0, root to right? Wait if left positive, right negative, as mid increases, want where becomes <=0. If hm>0, root to right => right? Let's define:
```
def _bisect_h_posneg(left,right,h_left,h_right,...): # h_left>0,h_right<=0
  for:
    mid; hm
    if hm > 0: left=mid  # still positive, root to the right, so increase left
    else: right=mid
```
Yes for both, if looking for transition from negative to positive (left neg, right pos): if hm>0 (positive), root is to left or right? Example h negative at 0, positive at 1. mid=0.5 if h>0, root between 0 and0.5 -> set right=mid. If h<0 set left=mid. So:
neg->pos: if hm>0: right=mid else left=mid.
pos->neg: if hm>0: left=mid else right=mid.
Need ensure our earlier logic reversed. Let's set correctly:
- `_bisect_h_negpos(left neg, right pos)`: if hm>0: right=mid else left=mid.
- `_bisect_h_posneg(left pos, right neg)`: if hm>0: left=mid else right=mid.
Good.

def make_perturbed(h,n,scale,rng,mode):
  x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
  if mode==0: h2=h + scale*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
  elif mode==1: h2=h + 2.0*scale*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*(1.0+rng.rand()*3.0)*x + rng.rand()*2.0*np.pi)
  elif mode==2: h2=h * np.exp(0.2*scale*rng.normal(0,1,n))? Simpler h + scale*rng.normal
  elif mode==3: block=(n//8); if block<2 block=2; start=int(rng.rand()*(n-block)); h2=h.copy(); h2[start:start+block]=1.0-h2[start:start+block]
  else: h2=h.copy()
  return project_feasible(h2,n)
```

Let's implement project_feasible robustly but concise.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(50):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            mask=h<1.0-1e-12
            if not np.any(mask): break
            room=float((1.0-h[mask]).sum())
            if room<=1e-15: break
            h[mask]=np.clip(h[mask]+diff/room*(1.0-h[mask]),0.0,1.0)
        else:
            mask=h>1e-12
            if not np.any(mask): break
            mass=float(h[mask].sum())
            if mass<=1e-15: break
            h[mask]=np.clip(h[mask]+diff/mass*h[mask],0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        idx=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12))[0]
        if idx.size==0:
            h+=diff/float(n)
            h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
            continue
        j=idx[0]
        newv=h[j]+diff
        if newv<0.0 or newv>1.0:
            # distribute over a few
            idxs = idx[:min(len(idx), max(1, int(abs(diff)*1e6)? no)
```
Need simpler final: If diff small and no slack, uniform. If diff small, one adjustment works if interior. If idx[0] has enough room? If newv out of [0,1], diff larger than slack; but after main loop diff likely tiny. We can do:
```
        if idx.size:
           h[idx[0]] = float(np.clip(h[idx[0]]+diff,0,1))
```
If clip changes sum, loop again. If all at bounds but feasible target, diff should be near0; uniform tiny.
Final maybe sum within 1e-13. Good.

But if n even target integer; if h all 0/1 sum off by integer? Main loop should fix by adjusting all proportionally until feasible. Good.

def _coord_e(base_o,a_o,const,d):
    if base_o.size:
        v=float(np.max(base_o + a_o*d))
        return v if v>const else const
    return const

`_coord_f`: calls e twice? We can compute p/e inline to avoid call overhead? Since used scalar many times. Define:
```
def _coord_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,d):
    if base_o.size:
       e=float(np.max(base_o+a_o*d))
       if e<const: e=const
    else: e=const
    p=base0+a0*d-d*d
    return p if p>e else e
```

def _coord_grid_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,dgrid):
    if base_o.size:
       vals=base_o[None,:]+a_o[None,:]*dgrid[:,None]
       e=np.max(vals,axis=1)
       if const > -1e299: e=np.maximum(e,const)
    else: e=np.full(dgrid.size,const)
    p=base0+a0*dgrid-dgrid*dgrid
    return np.maximum(e,p), p-e
```

```
def subgradient_phase(h,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=1500,topk=16,step0=0.04):
   h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
   c=compute_c5(h,n); best=h.copy(); bestc=c; mom=zeros; step=step0; noimp=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      carr=compute_corr(h); maxc=carr.max(); curr=maxc*2/n
      if curr < bestc - 1e-12:
         best=...; noimp=0
      else: noimp +=1
         if noimp > 250:
            # reset near best with noise
            h=make_perturbed(best,n,0.01,rng,it%4)
            mom=np.zeros(n); noimp=0
            continue? We need compute carr? Could continue to next iteration after perturbed.
      k=topk
      if carr.size < k: k=carr.size
      idx=np.argpartition(carr, -k)[-k:] if k>0 else ...
      grad=np.zeros(n)
      for m in idx: grad += _lag_grad(int(m),h,n)
      grad /= float(k)
      mom = 0.7*mom +0.3*grad
      step = step0 / (1.0 + 0.002*it)
      h_tri = h - step*mom
      h = project_feasible(h_tri,n)
   if curr < bestc: best...
   return best,bestc
```
If noimp reset, continue without gradient. Need update curr? Fine. But if time runs out after reset, best still.
`argpartition` if k=0? no. k min topk, carr.size. If topk > len, k=len. For speed topk maybe 8.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time()
  end=t0+budget_s-8.0
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
  except Exception: h=np.full(512,0.5); n=512
  if n < 16: ...? parent 700.
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n; best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
  # main cd
  dl=max(t0+5,end-350)
  if dl>t0:
    hh,cc=cd_exact(best_h,best_n,dl,max_passes=40,tol=1e-10)
    if cc<best_c: best_h=hh; best_c=cc; best_n=len(hh)
  # subgrad
  dl=end-300
  if dl>time.time()+20:
    hh,cc=subgradient_phase(best_h,best_n,dl,rng,max_iters=1200,topk=12)
    if cc<best_c: ...
  # cd polish
  dl=end-250
  if dl>time.time()+10:
    hh,cc=cd_exact(...)
    if cc<best_c: ...
  # restarts
  dl=end-150
  attempts=0
  while time.time()<dl and attempts<24:
     attempts+=1
     scale=0.004 + 0.05*(attempts/24.0)
     mode=attempts%4
     hp=make_perturbed(best_h,best_n,scale,rng,mode)
     sub=max(time.time()+5, min(dl, time.time()+35))
     hh,cc=cd_exact(hp,best_n,sub,max_passes=8,tol=1e-9)
     if cc<best_c:
        best_h=hh; best_c=cc; best_n=len(hh)
        # extra polish
        sub2=min(dl, time.time()+45)
        if sub2>time.time()+5:
           hh,cc=cd_exact(best_h,best_n,sub2,max_passes=10)
           if cc<best_c: best...
  # resample
  dl=end-70
  n_cur=best_n
  # generate unique n_try
  trials=[]
  base=float(n_cur)
  for mult in (0.7,0.85,1.0,1.2,1.4):
      trials.append(int(round(base*mult)))
  for extra in (400,600,800,1000,1200,1500,2000): trials.append(extra)
  # filter 128..2500 and unique
  for nt in trials:
     if time.time() > dl - 30: break
     if nt < 128 or nt > 2500 or nt == best_n: continue
     x_o=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,best_n)
     x_n=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nt)
     h2=project_feasible(np.interp(x_n,x_o,best_h),nt)
     c2=compute_c5(h2,nt)
     if c2 < best_c + 0.004:
        sub=min(dl, time.time()+50)
        if sub>time.time()+5:
           h2,c2=cd_exact(h2,nt,sub,max_passes=8)
     if c2<best_c:
       best_h=h2; best_c=c2; best_n=nt
  # final polishes
  for pass_count in (12,30):
     dl=end-2
     if time.time()>dl-10: break
     hh,cc=cd_exact(best_h,best_n,dl,max_passes=pass_count)
     if cc<best_c: best_h=hh; best_c=cc; best_n=len(hh)
  best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
  best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
  return best_h, best_c, best_n
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')

def c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(corr(h)) * (2.0/float(n)))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(60):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            m=h<1.0-1e-12
            if not np.any(m): break
            r=float((1.0-h[m]).sum())
            if r>1e-15: h[m]=np.clip(h[m]+diff/r*(1.0-h[m]),0.0,1.0)
            else: break
        else:
            m=h>1e-12
            if not np.any(m): break
            s=float(h[m].sum())
            if s>1e-15: h[m]=np.clip(h[m]+diff/s*h[m],0.0,1.0)
            else: break
    for _ in range(30):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        idx=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12))[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx[0]]=float(np.clip(h[idx[0]]+diff,0.0,1.0))
        else:
            h=np.clip(h+diff/float(n),0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
~25 lines.

Coord helpers:
```
def _e(base_o,a_o,const,d):
    if base_o.size:
        v=float(np.max(base_o+a_o*d))
        return v if v>const else const
    return const

def _f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,d):
    e=_e(base_o,a_o,const,d)
    p=base0+a0*d-d*d
    return p if p>e else e

def _hroot(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,d):
    e=_e(base_o,a_o,const,d)
    return base0+a0*d-d*d-e
```
Use calls to _e; overhead scalar only. OK.

def _bisect_np(lo,ro,ho, base...): # output variable names careful notshadow? We'll name left,r.

def _bisect_negpos(left,right,h_left,h_right,base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const):
    for _ in range(35):
        mid=0.5*(left+right)
        if _hroot(...,mid) > 0.0:
            right=mid
        else:
            left=mid
    return 0.5*(left+right)

def _bisect_posneg(...):
    ... if >0: left=mid else right=mid
```

`_grid_f`:
```
def _grid_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,dg):
    if base_o.size:
        vals=base_o[None,:]+a_o[None,:]*dg[:,None]
        e=np.max(vals,axis=1)
        if const > -1e299:
            e=np.maximum(e,const)
    else:
        e=np.full(dg.size,const)
    p=base0+a0*dg-dg*dg
    return np.maximum(e,p), p-e
```

`_coord_best_d`:
```
def _coord_best_d(base,a,idx0,const,h0):
    lo=-float(h0); hi=1.0-float(h0)
    base_o=base.copy(); a_o=a.copy(); base_o[idx0]=-1e300
    base0=float(base[idx0]); a0=float(a[idx0])
    if hi-lo <= 1e-14:
        return 0.0, _f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,0.0)
    lg=lo; hg=hi
    for _ in range(45):
        if hg-lg < 1e-11: break
        mid=0.5*(lg+hg)
        if base_o.size:
            vals=base_o+a_o*mid
            ml=float(np.max(vals))
            mv=ml if ml>const else const
            feas=vals >= mv-1e-12
            if np.any(feas):
                ms=float(a_o[feas].min()); Ms=float(a_o[feas].max())
            else:
                ms=0.0; Ms=0.0
        else:
            mv=const; ms=0.0; Ms=0.0
        if const >= mv-1e-12:
            if ms>0.0: ms=0.0
            if Ms<0.0: Ms=0.0
        if Ms < -1e-12: lg=mid
        elif ms > 1e-12: hg=mid
        else: break
    dg=0.5*(lg+hg)
    best_d=0.0; best_f=_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,0.0)
    for d in (dg,lo,hi):
        f0=_f(base_o,a_o,base0,a0,const,d)
        if f0<best_f: best_d=d; best_f=f0
    G=33 if base_o.size>100 else 17
    dgrid=np.linspace(lo,hi,G)
    fg,hg2=_grid_f(...)
    ib=int(np.argmin(fg)); 
    if fg[ib] < best_f: ...
    pos=np.where(hg2 > 1e-14)[0]
    if pos.size:
       fr=...
       if pos[0]>0: root=_bisect_negpos(...); update
       if pos[-1]<G-1: root=_bisect_posneg(...); update
    span=max(1e-6, (hi-lo)/float(G)*0.8)
    dref=np.clip(np.linspace(best_d-span,best_d+span,25),lo,hi)
    fr,_=_grid_f(...)
    ir=int(np.argmin(fr)); update
    best_d=float(np.clip(best_d,lo,hi))
    return best_d,best_f
```

`cd_exact`:
```
def cd_exact(h,n,deadline,max_passes=20,tol=1e-10):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=corr(h); cmax=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_cc=cmax
    n1=n-1; target=0.5*n; total=2*n-2
    ps=np.arange(n); K=np.maximum(ps,n1-ps); mlo_arr=n1-K; mhi_arr=n1+K
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        h_work=h.copy(); c_work=c.copy(); curr=cmax; improved=False; old=cmax
        for p in range(n):
            if time.time()>deadline: return best_h,best_cc*2.0/n
            h0=float(h_work[p])
            mlo=int(mlo_arr[p]); mhi=int(mhi_arr[p])
            base=c_work[mlo:mhi+1]; L=base.size
            r=n1-np.arange(mlo,mhi+1)
            a=np.zeros(L)
            fp=p+r; vf=(fp>=0)&(fp<n)
            if np.any(vf): a[vf]+=1.0-h_work[fp[vf]]
            bp=p-r; vb=(bp>=0)&(bp<n)
            if np.any(vb): a[vb]-=h_work[bp[vb]]
            idx0=int(n1-mlo)
            const=-1e300
            if mlo>0:
                v=float(c_work[:mlo].max()); const=v if v>const else const
            if mhi<total:
                v=float(c_work[mhi+1:].max()); const=v if v>const else const
            d,f_opt=_coord_best_d(base,a,idx0,const,h0)
            if f_opt < curr - tol:
                hv=float(np.clip(h0+d,0.0,1.0))
                h_work[p]=hv; d=float(hv-h0)? Wait if clip changes d? hv based on h0+d; use actual dd=hv-h0.
                newvals=base+a*dd; newvals[idx0]-=dd*dd
                c_work[mlo:mhi+1]=newvals
                curr=f_opt? If d clipped, f_opt for clipped maybe not f_opt. We should recompute f_opt for actual dd if different. If d within bounds by _coord_best_d, clip no change. To be safe: if dd != d: f_opt=_f? But need base_o etc. Simpler ensure d clipped inside by _coord_best_d. We'll trust. Use dd=d.
                h_work[p]=h0+d
                newvals...
                curr=f_opt
                improved=True
            if p+1==n or (p+1)%120==0:
                if abs(float(h_work.sum())-target) > 0.05*n:
                    h_work=project_feasible(h_work,n)
                    c_work=corr(h_work); curr=float(c_work.max())
        if not improved: break
        h_proj=project_feasible(h_work,n)
        c_proj=corr(h_proj); cp=float(c_proj.max())
        if cp < best_cc - 1e-12:
            best_h=h_proj.copy(); best_cc=cp
        if cp > cmax + 1e-8:
            break
        h=h_proj.copy(); c=c_proj.copy(); cmax=cp
        if not improved: break
    return best_h,best_cc*2.0/n
```
Need if d clipped: In `_coord_best_d`, return clipped, so OK.
`f_opt` if d at bound maybe. Good.

One issue: If we recompute `h_work`/`c_work` due sum drift inside coordinate loop, `curr` updated but `best_h` not. Fine.

Subgradient:
```
def lag_grad(m,h,n):
    n1=n-1; r=n1-int(m); g=np.zeros(n)
    if r>=0:
      g[:n-r]+=1.0-h[r:]
      g[r:]-=h[:n-r]
    else:
      a=-r; g[a:]+=1.0-h[:n-a]; g[:a]-=h[a:]
    return g

def subgradient_phase(h,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=1200,topk=12,step0=0.04):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute...; best=h.copy(); bestc=c; mom=np.zeros(n); no=0
    dx=2.0/n
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline break
       carr=corr(h); maxc=float(carr.max()); curr=maxc*dx
       if curr<bestc-1e-12: best=...; no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          if no>200:
             h=make_perturbed(best,n,0.01,rng,it%4); mom=np.zeros(n); no=0; continue
       k=topk
       if k>=carr.size: idx=np.arange(carr.size)
       else: idx=np.argpartition(carr,-k)[-k:]
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for m in idx: grad+=lag_grad(int(m),h,n)
       grad /= float(idx.size)
       mom=0.7*mom+0.3*grad
       step=step0/(1.0+0.003*it)
       h=project_feasible(h-step*mom,n)
    if float(corr(h).max()*dx)<bestc: best=h.copy(); bestc=...
    return best,bestc
```
Note `make_perturbed` may use project; defined before subgradient? It uses project, no issue if top-level order? In Python, functions can call later-defined if called at runtime after all definitions. OK.

`make_perturbed`:
```
def make_perturbed(h,n,scale,rng,mode):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    if mode==0: h2=h+scale*rng.normal(0,1,n)
    elif mode==1: h2=h+2.0*scale*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*(1.0+rng.rand()*3.0)*x+rng.rand()*2.0*np.pi)
    elif mode==2: h2=h*(1.0+0.5*scale*rng.normal(0,1,n))
    else:
       block=max(2,n//8); start=int(rng.rand()*(n-block+1)); h2=h.copy(); h2[start:start+block]=1.0-h2[start:start+block]
    return project_feasible(h2,n)
```
For mode2, h*(1+noise) can negative? project.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(corr(h)) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            m = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
            if not np.any(m):
                break
            room = float((1.0 - h[m]).sum())
            if room > 1e-15:
                h[m] = np.clip(h[m] + (diff / room) * (1.0 - h[m]), 0.0, 1.0)
            else:
                break
        else:
            m = h > 1e-12
            if not np.any(m):
                break
            mass = float(h[m].sum())
            if mass > 1e-15:
                h[m] = np.clip(h[m] + (diff / mass) * h[m], 0.0, 1.0)
            else:
                break
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        idx = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx[0]] = float(np.clip(h[idx[0]] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
        else:
            h = np.clip(h + diff / float(n), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def _e(base_o, a_o, const, d):
    if base_o.size:
        v = float(np.max(base_o + a_o * d))
        return v if v > const else const
    return const


def _f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, d):
    e = _e(base_o, a_o, const, d)
    p = base0 + a0 * d - d * d
    return p if p > e else e


def _hroot(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, d):
    e = _e(base_o, a_o, const, d)
    return base0 + a0 * d - d * d - e


def _bisect_negpos(left, right, base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const):
    for _ in range(35):
        mid = 0.5 * (left + right)
        if _hroot(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, mid) > 0.0:
            right = mid
        else:
            left = mid
    return 0.5 * (left + right)


def _bisect_posneg(left, right, base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const):
    for _ in range(35):
        mid = 0.5 * (left + right)
        if _hroot(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, mid) > 0.0:
            left = mid
        else:
            right = mid
    return 0.5 * (left + right)


def _grid_f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, dg):
    if base_o.size:
        vals = base_o[None, :] + a_o[None, :] * dg[:, None]
        e = np.max(vals, axis=1)
        if const > -1e299:
            e = np.maximum(e, const)
    else:
        e = np.full(dg.size, const)
    p = base0 + a0 * dg - dg * dg
    return np.maximum(e, p), p - e


def _coord_best_d(base, a, idx0, const, h0):
    lo = -float(h0)
    hi = 1.0 - float(h0)
    base_o = base.copy()
    a_o = a.copy()
    base_o[idx0] = -1e300
    base0 = float(base[idx0])
    a0 = float(a[idx0])
    if hi - lo <= 1e-14:
        return 0.0, _f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, 0.0)

    lg = lo
    hg = hi
    for _ in range(45):
        if hg - lg < 1e-11:
            break
        mid = 0.5 * (lg + hg)
        if base_o.size:
            vals = base_o + a_o * mid
            ml = float(np.max(vals))
            mv = ml if ml > const else const
            feas = vals >= mv - 1e-12
            if np.any(feas):
                ms = float(a_o[feas].min())
                Ms = float(a_o[feas].max())
            else:
                ms = 0.0
                Ms = 0.0
        else:
            mv = const
            ms = 0.0
            Ms = 0.0
        if const >= mv - 1e-12:
            if ms > 0.0:
                ms = 0.0
            if Ms < 0.0:
                Ms = 0.0
        if Ms < -1e-12:
            lg = mid
        elif ms > 1e-12:
            hg = mid
        else:
            break

    dg = 0.5 * (lg + hg)
    best_d = 0.0
    best_f = _f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, 0.0)
    for d in (dg, lo, hi):
        f0 = _f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, d)
        if f0 < best_f:
            best_d = float(d)
            best_f = f0

    G = 33 if base_o.size > 100 else 17
    dgrid = np.linspace(lo, hi, G)
    fgrid, hgrid = _grid_f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, dgrid)
    ib = int(np.argmin(fgrid))
    if fgrid[ib] < best_f:
        best_d = float(dgrid[ib])
        best_f = float(fgrid[ib])

    pos = np.where(hgrid > 1e-14)[0]
    if pos.size:
        first = int(pos[0])
        last = int(pos[-1])
        if first > 0:
            root = _bisect_negpos(float(dgrid[first - 1]), float(dgrid[first]),
                                  base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const)
            f0 = _f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, root)
            if f0 < best_f:
                best_d = float(root)
                best_f = f0
        if last < G - 1:
            root = _bisect_posneg(float(dgrid[last]), float(dgrid[last + 1]),
                                  base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const)
            f0 = _f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, root)
            if f0 < best_f:
                best_d = float(root)
                best_f = f0

    span = max(1e-6, (hi - lo) / float(G) * 0.8)
    dref = np.clip(np.linspace(best_d - span, best_d + span, 25), lo, hi)
    fref, _ = _grid_f(base_o, a_o, base0, a0, const, dref)
    ir = int(np.argmin(fref))
    if fref[ir] < best_f:
        best_d = float(dref[ir])
        best_f = float(fref[ir])

    best_d = float(np.clip(best_d, lo, hi))
    return best_d, best_f


def cd_exact(h, n, deadline, max_passes=20, tol=1e-10):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = corr(h)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cc = cmax
    n1 = n - 1
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    total = 2 * n - 2
    ps = np.arange(n)
    K = np.maximum(ps, n1 - ps)
    mlo_arr = n1 - K
    mhi_arr = n1 + K

    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h_work = h.copy()
        c_work = c.copy()
        curr = cmax
        old = curr
        improved = False

        for p in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                return best_h, best_cc * (2.0 / float(n))
            h0 = float(h_work[p])
            mlo = int(mlo_arr[p])
            mhi = int(mhi_arr[p])
            base = c_work[mlo:mhi + 1]
            r = n1 - np.arange(mlo, mhi + 1)
            a = np.zeros(base.size)
            fp = p + r
            vf = (fp >= 0) & (fp < n)
            if np.any(vf):
                a[vf] += 1.0 - h_work[fp[vf]]
            bp = p - r
            vb = (bp >= 0) & (bp < n)
            if np.any(vb):
                a[vb] -= h_work[bp[vb]]
            idx0 = int(n1 - mlo)
            const = -1e300
            if mlo > 0:
                v = float(c_work[:mlo].max())
                if v > const:
                    const = v
            if mhi < total:
                v = float(c_work[mhi + 1:].max())
                if v > const:
                    const = v

            d, f_opt = _coord_best_d(base, a, idx0, const, h0)
            if f_opt < curr - tol:
                h_work[p] = float(np.clip(h0 + d, 0.0, 1.0))
                newvals = base + a * d
                newvals[idx0] -= d * d
                c_work[mlo:mhi + 1] = newvals
                curr = float(c_work.max())
                improved = True

            if (p + 1 == n) or ((p + 1) % 120 == 0):
                if abs(float(h_work.sum()) - target) > 0.05 * float(n):
                    h_work = project_feasible(h_work, n)
                    c_work = corr(h_work)
                    curr = float(c_work.max())

        if not improved:
            break

        h_proj = project_feasible(h_work, n)
        c_proj = corr(h_proj)
        cp = float(c_proj.max())
        if cp < best_cc - 1e-12:
            best_h = h_proj.copy()
            best_cc = cp
        if cp > old + 1e-8:
            break
        h = h_proj.copy()
        c = c_proj.copy()
        cmax = cp

    return best_h, best_cc * (2.0 / float(n))


def lag_grad(m, h, n):
    n1 = n - 1
    r = n1 - int(m)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    if r >= 0:
        g[:n - r] += 1.0 - h[r:]
        g[r:] -= h[:n - r]
    else:
        a = -r
        g[a:] += 1.0 - h[:n - a]
        g[:a] -= h[a:]
    return g


def make_perturbed(h, n, scale, rng, mode):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    if mode == 0:
        h2 = h + scale * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    elif mode == 1:
        h2 = h + 2.0 * scale * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (1.0 + rng.rand() * 3.0) * x + rng.rand() * 2.0 * np.pi)
    elif mode == 2:
        h2 = h * (1.0 + 0.5 * scale * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    else:
        block = max(2, n // 8)
        start = int(rng.rand() * (n - block + 1))
        h2 = h.copy()
        h2[start:start + block] = 1.0 - h2[start:start + block]
    return project_feasible(h2, n)


def subgradient_phase(h, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=1200, topk=12, step0=0.04):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = c5(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    mom = np.zeros(n)
    no_imp = 0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        carr = corr(h)
        maxc = float(carr.max())
        curr = maxc * dx
        if curr < bestc - 1e-12:
            best = h.copy()
            bestc = curr
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp > 200:
                h = make_perturbed(best, n, 0.01, rng, it % 4)
                mom = np.zeros(n)
                no_imp = 0
                continue
        k = int(topk)
        if k >= carr.size:
            idx = np.arange(carr.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(carr, -k)[-k:]
        g = np.zeros(n)
        for m in idx:
            g += lag_grad(int(m), h, n)
        g /= float(idx.size)
        mom = 0.7 * mom + 0.3 * g
        step = step0 / (1.0 + 0.003 * it)
        h = project_feasible(h - step * mom, n)
    last = c5(h, n)
    if last < bestc:
        best = h.copy()
        bestc = last
    return best, bestc


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    end = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h)
    except Exception:
        h = np.full(512, 0.5)
        n = 512

    if n < 300:
        n2 = 700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2)
        h = project_feasible(np.interp(xn, x, h), n2)
        n = n2

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_n = n
    best_c = c5(h, n)

    dl = end - 350.0
    if dl > time.time() + 10.0:
        hh, cc = cd_exact(best_h, best_n, dl, 40, 1e-10)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = len(hh)
            best_c = cc

    dl = end - 300.0
    if dl > time.time() + 20.0:
        hh, cc = subgradient_phase(best_h, best_n, dl, rng, 1200, 12)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = len(hh)
            best_c = cc

    dl = end - 260.0
    if dl > time.time() + 10.0:
        hh, cc = cd_exact(best_h, best_n, dl, 20, 1e-11)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = len(hh)
            best_c = cc

    dl = end - 150.0
    attempts = 0
    while time.time() < dl and attempts < 20:
        attempts += 1
        scale = 0.004 + 0.05 * (float(attempts) / 20.0)
        hp = make_perturbed(best_h, best_n, scale, rng, attempts % 4)
        sub = min(dl, time.time() + 35.0)
        hh, cc = cd_exact(hp, best_n, sub, 8, 1e-9)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = len(hh)
            best_c = cc
            sub2 = min(dl, time.time() + 45.0)
            if sub2 > time.time() + 5.0:
                hh, cc = cd_exact(best_h, best_n, sub2, 12, 1e-10)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_n = len(hh)
                    best_c = cc

    dl = end - 70.0
    seen = set()
    trials = []
    for mult in (0.7, 0.85, 1.0, 1.2, 1.45):
        trials.append(int(round(float(best_n) * mult)))
    for ex in (400, 600, 800, 1000, 1200, 1400, 1600):
        trials.append(ex)

    for nt in trials:
        if time.time() > dl - 25.0:
            break
        if nt < 120 or nt > 1800 or nt in seen or nt == best_n:
            continue
        seen.add(nt)
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nt)
        h2 = project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, best_h), nt)
        c2 = c5(h2, nt)
        if c2 < best_c + 0.004:
            sub = min(dl, time.time() + 45.0)
            if sub > time.time() + 5.0:
                h2, c2 = cd_exact(h2, nt, sub, 8, 1e-9)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_n = nt
            best_c = c2

    for mp in (10, 25):
        dl = end - 2.0
        if time.time() > dl - 8.0:
            break
        hh, cc = cd_exact(best_h, best_n, dl, mp, 1e-11)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = len(hh)
            best_c = cc

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
